#### Corridor: components of the macro error

- Components of the macro-error vector per law, signed relative errors against the ground truth (1370 runs (I-80: 26 of 26 laws with runs, 3 scenarios; US-101: 21 of 21 laws with runs, 3 scenarios; 10 seeds per scenario); unit run; 95 % percentile bootstrap intervals, 1000 resamples); anchored: follow the demand and the downstream boundary of the data; dynamic: decided by the law. FD: RMSE of the binned flow / mean flow of the truth; waves: (run - truth) / max(truth, 1); travel time: Wasserstein-1 / mean of the truth; wave speed: cross-correlation.

- H12.2, residual_idm_certified (I-80 and US-101): **open**; fails: macro part (TOST) and micro part; macro (TOST +-10 %): I-80: throughput -1.1 % [-1.4 %, -0.7 %] equivalent, travel time -1.2 % [-1.5 %, -1.0 %] equivalent, wave speed +30.5 % [+23.1 %, +38.2 %] not equivalent (faster); US-101: throughput +0.0 % [-0.1 %, +0.2 %] equivalent, travel time -6.5 % [-7.2 %, -5.9 %] equivalent, wave speed -7.1 % [-25.0 %, +13.9 %] not equivalent (slower); micro: RMSE vs idm +4.8 % [+3.7 %, +5.9 %] over 5521 drivers, not superior.

## 9. Deviations from the specification

127 D-entries of `docs/decisions.md`, the first sentence of each decision, by milestone: the one of its section in the log, or a later one when the entry's number is at or above the first new entry that a later milestone report cites (the entries are numbered in time).

### M1 (data pipeline, IDM calibration, persistence baseline)

- D1 (2026-09-27): Positions are defined at the bumpers that face each other: `x_lead` = leader rear bumper, `x_follower` = follower front bumper, so `s = x_lead - x_follower` holds exactly.
- D2 (2026-09-27): For sources without a common longitudinal coordinate (OpenACC, FollowNet) `x_lead` is the trapezoidal integral of `v_lead` and `x_follower = x_lead - s`.
- D3 (2026-09-27): Stored speeds are the measured ones; the Savitzky-Golay filter is used only to compute the acceleration.
- D4 (2026-09-27): "Follower speed > 0" is evaluated as `v > 0.1` m/s.
- D5 (2026-09-27): Duration criterion is `n * dt >= 15` s: at least 150 samples at 10 Hz.
- D6 (2026-09-27): Samples with `s <= 0` or `s >= 120` m end a segment instead of discarding the whole pair.
- D7 (2026-09-27): Long runs are cut into chunks of at most `max_duration` (OpenACC: 60 s; other datasets: no limit).
- D8 (2026-09-27): One split per dataset and kind (seed 0); validation fold = test fold + 1.
- D9 (2026-09-27): NRMSE is RMSE divided by the root mean square of the observed series (Punzo et al.
- D10 (2026-09-27): Differential evolution is implemented in torch and batched over events (GPU), with the default settings of `scipy.optimize.differential_evolution` except for the final polishing step, which is omitted.
- D11 (2026-09-27): Collision during calibration: `J = 10 + share of samples with s <= 0`.
- D12 (2026-09-27): Global parameters minimise the NRMSE of spacing and speed pooled over all events plus `10 x collision rate`.
- D13 (2026-09-27): Persistence model derives `a(t)` from the state history as `(v[t] - v[t-dt]) / dt` (window 2).
- D14 (2026-09-27): Deviation from the specification text: OpenACC `IVS_i` is read as the gap between vehicle `i` and vehicle `i + 1` (spacing of follower `i + 1`), not as "the spacing between vehicle i and its leader".
- D15 (2026-09-27): OpenACC "driver" for the driver-level split is the pair (vehicle model, driving mode): `follower_id = openacc/<campaign>/<vehicle>:<ACC or Human or unknown>`.
- D16 (2026-09-27): Driving mode of an OpenACC sample comes from the `Driver<i>` column; if the column is absent, from the header flag (1 -> ACC, 0 -> Human, otherwise `unknown`).
- D17 (2026-09-27): FollowNet relative speed is negated at load time (`dv = v - v_lead = -(v_lead - v)`).
- D18 (2026-09-27): FollowNet NGSIM I-80 and Waymo spacing is reduced by a nominal vehicle length of 4.5 m (`spacing_offset`), HighD by 0.
- D19 (2026-09-27): FollowNet events have no identifiers: every event is its own driver (`follower_id = <dataset>/<site>/ev<index>`), one site per file.
- D20 (2026-09-27): FollowNet HighD is resampled from 25 Hz to 10 Hz by linear interpolation without an anti-aliasing filter.
- D21 (2026-09-27): Pickled FollowNet files (object arrays) are loaded only after their sha256 matches `SOURCES.json` written by `scripts/download_data.py`, and are cached as plain arrays (`.npz`, no pickle).
- D22 (2026-09-27): Deviation from the specification: raw highD is not available (no levelXdata agreement yet; confirmed by the user on 2026-09-27).
- D23 (2026-09-27): Waymo pairs: bumper positions are derived from the box centres and the vehicle lengths of the file; all Waymo events belong to one site (`all`); every event whose follower is the AV has the same driver id `waymo/all/AV`.
- D24 (2026-09-27): The Waymo pair files were downloaded with PowerShell `Invoke-WebRequest` (sha256 verified against the Mendeley API).
- D25 (2026-09-27): `resample_uniform` sorts the samples by time, keeps the first of repeated time stamps, never extrapolates; grid points inside a source gap longer than 0.35 s are NaN.
- D26 (2026-09-27): Savitzky-Golay window = odd number of samples nearest to `window_s / dt` (11 at 10 Hz); `central_diff` uses second-order one-sided differences at the ends.
- D27 (2026-09-27): The time grid has a gap where a step differs from `dt` by more than 0.1 %; event time is rebuilt as `k * dt`.
- D28 (2026-09-27): Measured speeds in (-0.1, 0) m/s are set to 0; speeds <= -0.1 m/s and NaN dataset accelerations invalidate the sample.
- D29 (2026-09-27): The acceleration is computed on each maximal valid run before chunking; the moving-fraction filter is applied to each chunk.
- D30 (2026-09-27): With `smooth_speeds=true` the stored speeds are smoothed over the whole run, the acceleration still comes from the measured speed.
- D31 (2026-09-27): `filter_event` keeps the acceleration of the pre-segmented event; ids get a suffix `_<j>` only when the event is split.
- D32 (2026-09-27): Split groups are sorted before shuffling; a group goes to the currently lightest fold (weight = samples).
- D33 (2026-09-27): `config_hash` of an extraction covers the data config and the effective extraction options, not the paths and not the split settings; numbers are hashed as floats (60 and 60.0 are the same setting).
- D34 (2026-09-27): Stopping rule of the differential evolution: per event `std(J) <= 1e-4 + 1e-3 *
- D35 (2026-09-27): The acceleration stored in an event is the instantaneous acceleration at the sample (central difference), while the rollout applies `a[k]` over `[k, k + 1]` (semi-implicit Euler).
- D36 (2026-09-27): Closed-loop metrics include the first sample (whose error is zero by construction); a model with window `W` is scored from index `W - 1`.
- D37 (2026-09-27): OpenACC data files are recognised by a first line starting with `Date` (skips the vehicle specification tables and the ZalaZone list of long-gap vehicles); an empty or unknown `Driver` cell is `unknown`.
- D38 (2026-09-27): highD: a 10 Hz sample that lies between two frames with different leaders or lanes gets no leader state, so states of two leaders are never interpolated.
- D39 (2026-09-27): Waymo pairs: both vehicles are put on one 10 Hz grid starting at the earlier first sample (the file has 584 steps of 0.09 or 0.11 s).
- D40 (2026-09-27): Batch `i` of the per-event calibration uses the seed `seed + i`; the result therefore depends on `batch_events`, which is part of the hashed config.
- D41 (2026-09-28): Deviation from the specification, decided by the user at the M1 review: `extraction.min_spacing = 0.5` m; a sample is valid when `0.5 < s < 120` m.
- D42 (2026-09-27): NGSIM reconstruction = constrained smoothing of the longitudinal positions, one quadratic programme per track (OSQP): least squares to the observed positions with Huber weights (threshold 1 m, 2 re-weighting iterations) plus a squared-jerk penalty whose weight puts the gain 1/2 of the smoother at 0.5 Hz; hard constraints speed >= 0, acceleration in [-8, 4] m/s^2,
- D43 (2026-09-27): Leader frames whose constraint would push the follower back by more than 5 m (`max_push`) are left unconstrained.
- D44 (2026-09-27): Reconstructed speed, acceleration and jerk are centred differences of the reconstructed positions (`v[k]` = mean of the two adjacent interval speeds, `a[k]` = second difference at `k`), so that the state and the acceleration refer to the same instant; `a_source = "reconstruction"`.
- D45 (2026-09-27): NGSIM period = rank of `global_time - 100 * frame_id`; track key (period, vehicle_id); event lane = lane of the follower at the start of the event; mainline lanes only (I-80: 1-6 including the HOV lane; US-101: 1-5).
- D46 (2026-09-27): Macroscopic validation of the reconstruction: Edie's flow, density and speed on a 50 m x 10 s grid per lane and over the lanes, computed from the raw and from the reconstructed trajectories (trajectories split exactly at the grid lines); reported: relative difference of total distance and total time, RMSE and correlation of the cell speeds, relative error of flow and density over the cells with at least 5 vehicle-seconds.
- D47 (2026-09-27): The optimiser is run 3 times with independent seeds (`calibration.restarts`), per event the run with the lowest objective is kept; same for the global fit.
- D48 (2026-09-28): Decided by the user at the M1 review: the heterogeneous IDM of the corridor (M5) keeps `v0` and `b` fixed and estimates `T, s0, a` per event.
- D49 (2026-09-28): Decided by the user at the M1 review: the IDM of the I-80 corridor is calibrated on the events of `ngsim_i80` (own reconstruction), not on highD.
- D50 (2026-09-28): Minimum net gap of the NGSIM reconstruction raised from 0.5 to 0.75 m.

### M2 (models and closed-loop evaluation)

- D51 (2026-09-28): Every model standardises its inputs inside (`InputScaler`): callers pass raw SI states, autograd differentiates through the whole model.
- D52 (2026-09-28): The physics of PERL is the adapted Newell model of the PERL paper (Long et al.
- D53 (2026-09-28): PIDL: collocation states uniform in the box of the 1 % - 99 % quantiles of the training states, as many as the batch has samples, `alpha = 1`; the IDM acceleration of the physics term is clipped to [-8, 4] m/s^2.
- D54 (2026-09-28): Learnable IDM parameters (PIDL, ResidualIDM) are stored as logits and mapped into the calibration bounds by a sigmoid.
- D55 (2026-09-28): ResidualIDM: `r = r_max * tanh(lipschitz * g(x / scale))`, spectral normalisation on every linear layer of `g`, residual exactly zero at initialisation (duplicated hidden units with opposite output weights).
- D56 (2026-09-28): kNN uses at most 200 000 training samples (seeded draw), brute-force search in torch.
- D57 (2026-09-28): Closed-loop scoring of every model starts at sample 29: the first 30 samples (3 s) are observed warm-up, given to the model as history.
- D58 (2026-09-28): Extra model `ovm` (not in the specification): `a = (min(v0, (s - s0) / tau) - v) / tau`, calibrated globally like the IDM.
- D59 (2026-09-28): Training: rollout loss `MSE(s) / var_s + MSE(v) / var_v` (variances of the training samples) with weight 1 by default; one epoch = one pass over the window ends, at most 300 steps of 4096 samples; gradient norm clipped at 10; float32; early stopping on the mean closed-loop spacing RMSE of the validation events, patience 10.
- D60 (2026-09-28): The global calibrations of the training part of a fold (IDM for `idm`, `pidl`, `residual_idm`; wave speed for `newell`, `perl`; OVM) are cached in `data/calibration/<event set>/` under a hash of the event ids and the settings; all seeds of a fold share them.
- D61 (2026-09-28): The initial weights take part in the model selection as epoch 0 (validation closed-loop spacing RMSE before the first step), except in a training with a stability penalty, where only trained epochs are eligible (otherwise the unpenalised physics of a hybrid wins every selection in which the penalty costs accuracy: ResidualIDM with the Jacobian penalty returned its IDM).
- D62 (2026-09-28): Option `train.acc_weight` (weight of the one-step acceleration MSE, default 1 = specification).

### M3 (stability module)

- D63 (2026-09-28): Decided by the user at the M2 review: (1) the audited architectures are trained with the loss of the specification (`acc_weight = 1`), ResidualIDM with the rollout loss only (`acc_weight = 0`, set in `configs/model/residual_idm.yaml` through `train_overrides`), and MLP, GRU, LSTM are trained once more with `acc_weight = 0` on one fold as a sensitivity analysis; (2) the compute budget of E1 (about 19 GPU hours sequentially) and E2 is accepted; (3) the vehicles of the heterogeneous IDM of the corridor are drawn from the per-event estimates of `T, s0, a` that lie inside the bounds.
- D64 (2026-09-28): Deviation from the specification: frequencies are 25 log-spaced values from 0.02 to 2.0 rad/s (`numpy.geomspace`; `logspace(0.02, 2.0, 25)` read literally would be 1.05-100 rad/s).
- D65 (2026-09-28): Three stability flags are reported side by side: numerical (largest measured gain > 1.02, the rule of the specification), analytic gain (largest gain of the exact linearisation on the same frequencies > 1.02) and sign criterion (`f_v^2 + 2 f_v f_dv - 2 f_s < 0`).
- D66 (2026-09-28): Windowed models are analysed with the exact linearisation of the law with its history (`history_jacobian`, `transfer_windowed`); the memoryless view (derivatives summed over the window) is reported but describes only the limit of zero frequency.
- D67 (2026-09-28): Equilibria are searched on a scan of 400 spacings in [1, 200] m, the first upward zero crossing is refined by bisection; a law that is zero for every constant state (Newell, persistence) is `indifferent` and is excited at the nominal gap `2 m + 1.5 s * v`.
- D68 (2026-09-28): kNN: partial derivatives by central differences with steps of 0.25 standard deviations; the numerical frequency response is the authority for this model.
- D69 (2026-09-28): Penalties: speeds of the equilibria come from their own generator (the batches of a run with penalty equal those of the run without); `n_equilibria` 16 (Jacobian, linear gain) or 3 (gain); speeds without equilibrium are skipped and counted; a law that is indifferent is penalised at the nominal gap.
- D70 (2026-09-28): `LinearGainPenalty` (not in the specification): `relu(
- D71 (2026-09-28): Certificate of ResidualIDM in two forms: at the equilibria of the hybrid (a posteriori), and over all spacings that can be an equilibrium of any residual with `
- D72 (2026-09-28): Option `calibration.stability_margin` of the global IDM fit: the objective carries 10 x the shortfall of the analytic string-stability margin over the speeds 5-30 m/s (closed-form IDM derivatives).
- D73 (2026-09-28): Platoon test: vehicles start at the time gap `2 m + 1.5 s * v` by default, not at the equilibrium of the model; all vehicles of all profiles are stepped together.
- D74 (2026-09-28): Deviation from the specification: every penalty carries an existence term, `relu(f(1 m, 0, v) + m_e) + relu(m_e - f(200 m, 0, v))` with `m_e = 0.1` m/s^2, at the sampled speeds that have no equilibrium and lie inside the speed range of the training data (`existence_weight = 1`; 0 restores the specification).
- D75 (2026-09-28): The primary stability numbers of a model are the shares of the grid speeds (inside the data range) with a stable equilibrium, with an unstable equilibrium, and without equilibrium; they sum to 1.
- D76 (2026-09-28): The linearised gain penalty is evaluated at the 25 frequencies of the audit (0.02-2.0 rad/s, log-spaced) with the margin `m_g(w) = 0.05 * min(1, (w / 0.1 rad/s)^2)`; mean over equilibria and frequencies.
- D77 (2026-09-29): `git_revision()` reads the commit hash from the files of `.git` (HEAD, loose reference, `packed-refs`, work trees), without a child process.

### M4 (experiments E1-E5)

- D78 (2026-09-29): Spacing band of the data: for every grid speed, the 5 %, 50 % and 95 % quantiles of the spacing of the training samples with `
- D79 (2026-09-29): Deviation from the specification: the equilibrium of a speed is searched inside the band of that speed; an equilibrium that exists only outside the band is reported as `outside`.
- D80 (2026-09-29): Deviation from the specification: existence term at the edges of the band: at every sampled speed with a band, `relu(f(s_low, 0, v) + m_e) + relu(m_e - f(s_high, 0, v))`, `m_e = 0.1 m/s^2`.
- D81 (2026-09-29): The linearised gain penalty takes the maximum over the frequencies at every equilibrium (`aggregate: max`), then the mean over the equilibria.
- D82 (2026-09-29): Deviation from the specification: the experiments are run by a queue of separate processes (`scripts/run_experiment.py`, plain YAML in `configs/queue/`), not by hydra multirun.
- D83 (2026-09-29): Hysteresis loop area: area enclosed by the trajectory of the first follower in the plane (gap, speed) over the whole run, closed by the straight line back to the start, in m^2/s; headline number: the braking pulse.
- D84 (2026-09-29): Transfer (E3): models of seed 0 of every fold, without penalty and with the chosen weight, are evaluated on all events of `ngsim_i80`, `ngsim_us101` and `waymo`.
- D85 (2026-09-29): Reduced design of E2 (accepted proposal 4): weights 0.01, 0.1, 1, 10 on the five folds with seed 0; seeds 1-4 only for the chosen weight.
- D86 (2026-09-29): E4 (accepted proposal 3): IDM core calibrated with a lower bound of 0.2 on the margin; residual amplitude `r_max` chosen by a pilot on fold 0 among 0.1, 0.2, 0.3 and 0.5 m/s^2 (smallest validation RMSE of the trained hybrid); `lipschitz = 0.9 * b / r_max` with `b` the largest product for which the a priori certificate holds on the whole grid at that `r_max`.
- D87 (2026-09-29): OpenACC models (E5 and, by accepted proposal 5, H1.3): views `openacc_acc` and `openacc_human` of the event set (driving mode of the follower), both without the five run files that give the leader profiles of the platoon test.
- D88 (2026-09-29): Statistics: for RMSE-type numbers the unit of the bootstrap and of the paired tests is the driver (mean over the events of the driver and over the seeds); for numbers of a model (stability shares, growth error, hysteresis) the unit is the run (fold and seed).
- D89 (2026-09-29): Deviation from the specification: under a penalty the best epoch is the feasible epoch (penalty at the grid speeds at most 0.01) with the smallest validation RMSE; while no epoch is feasible, the epoch with the smallest validation RMSE, as the specification says.
- D90 (2026-09-29): Change of the accepted proposal 2 of the M3 review, back to the specification: in E2 the recurrent models (GRU, LSTM, PERL) are trained with the rollout gain penalty of the specification (`kind: gain`), not with the linearised one.
- D91 (2026-09-29): Platoon test: every vehicle starts at the equilibrium of the law inside the band at the first speed of the leader (`start_gap: anchored`); at the time gap `2 m + 1.5 s * v` of D73 only where the start speed has no band or the law has no equilibrium inside it.
- D92 (2026-09-29): Verdict of H1.1: on the share of unstable equilibria among the equilibria of the model at the speeds in support (the equilibrium inside the band where there is one, the first equilibrium of `[1, 200]` m otherwise), numerical rule; the band shares (D79) are reported next to it.
- D93 (2026-09-29): New arm of E2, `e2_existence`: GRU, LSTM and PERL with the existence term alone (`kind: existence`: an equilibrium inside the band at every speed, no stability term), five folds, seed 0.

### M5 (I-80 corridor)

- D94 (2026-09-30): I-80 scenario: corridor coordinate of the reconstruction; edges `main_a` [20, 100] m with 6 lanes, `main_b` [100, 204] m with 7 lanes (right-most = auxiliary lane of the on-ramp), `main_c` [204, 500] m with 6 lanes, buffer `out` [500, 650] m; every track of a period is one vehicle, inserted at the time, lane, position and speed of its first sample, with its length and class; vehicle types with `minGap = 0`, `sigma = 0`, LC2013 with SUMO's defaults except `lcKeepRight = 0`.
- D95 (2026-09-30): Downstream boundary: on the buffer edge the speed of every vehicle is prescribed: the speed of the data (mean of the samples at 470-500 m of the lane within 2.5 s) times a factor `g = clip(1 - 0.02 * dN, 0.2, 1.5)`, where `dN` is the number of simulated vehicles that have left the section minus the number of data vehicles that have left it by that time; the speed of a buffer vehicle changes by at most -3 / +2 m/s^2.
- D96 (2026-09-30): Control loop: SUMO's Euler update (position advanced by the new speed) with `setSpeed` under speed mode 0 is the integration scheme of the project, so the ballistic update and `setPreviousSpeed` of the specification are not used.
- D97 (2026-09-30): Laws of the corridor (accepted proposal 1 of the M4 review): 13 laws, all fitted to NGSIM I-80: `idm_global` (five fold calibrations), `idm_heterogeneous` (interior per-event estimates, D48), `knn`, and the HighD models of seed 0 fine-tuned on the NGSIM folds: `mlp`, `pidl`, `gru`, `lstm`, `perl`, `residual_idm`, `residual_idm_certified`, `mlp_penalty`, `gru_penalty`, `lstm_penalty` (the penalised ones continue with their penalty).
- D98 (2026-09-30): Macroscopic metrics (definitions in `docs/m5_contract.md`, section 5): throughput at 450 m; Edie mean speed; fundamental diagram on cells of 100 m x 30 s; queue discharge flow and capacity drop from the detectors at 100 m and 450 m; stop waves as connected regions of the speed field below 40 % of the local median, wave speed from their leading edges; travel time between 30 m and 490 m.
- D99 (2026-09-30): Macro-error vector: relative errors of throughput, mean speed, queue discharge flow, fundamental diagram, wave speed, number of waves, wave amplitude, travel time (Wasserstein-1 over the mean of the truth); `macro_error` = mean of the absolute components that exist.
- D100 (2026-09-30): Statistics of M5: unit = run (scenario and seed) for the metrics of a law; correlation (Spearman, Pearson) between the mean macro error of a law and the unstable-equilibrium fraction of its members over the laws, bootstrap over the laws; TOST of the certified hybrid against `idm_global` on paired runs with a margin of 10 %.
- D101 (2026-09-30): The I-24 builder is not written: `corridor.i24.enabled` exists, is false, and with true the build stops with the message that the I-24 MOTION data are not on this machine.
- D102 (2026-09-30): Collisions in the corridor keep the vehicle (contact instead of removal): a follower whose net gap becomes <= 0 is counted once per contact episode (a new episode after the gap has exceeded 1 m), stays in the run, and while its gap is below 1 m its commanded speed is capped so that it cannot penetrate further; outside contact the law alone drives it.
- D103 (2026-09-30): NGSIM lane 1 is an HOV lane: vehicles that use lane 1 at any time in the data get the class `hov`; lane 1 of the main-line edges admits `hov` only.
- D104 (2026-09-30): Insertion: SUMO's default insertion checks with vehicle types of `tau = 1.0 s`, `decel = 8 m/s^2`, `emergencyDecel = 8 m/s^2`; the delay of the entries is reported.

### M6 (report generation)

- D105 (2026-10-01): The macro error of M5 is reported by components, the anchored ones (throughput, mean speed, queue discharge, travel time) marked as such, and a dynamic macro error (mean of the absolute errors of the fundamental diagram, the wave speed by cross-correlation, the number of waves and the wave amplitude) is reported with its own correlation to the instability.
- D106 (2026-10-01): `scripts/make_report.py` writes `runs/_report/`: every table of M4 and M5 as CSV (full precision) and LaTeX (booktabs, rounded), seven figures as PNG and PDF, the generated `report.md` with the numbers taken from the tables, and a manifest with run counts and hashes.
- D107 (2026-10-01): The growth error and the standard-deviation ratio of a platoon that collides are undefined; the means of a run are taken over the profiles without collision; H1.3 and the growth-error comparison of E2 use the pairs in which both runs have a value, with the numbers of pairs and of collided profiles reported, and the verdict is "open" below three pairs.

### M7 (revision arms)

- D108 (2026-10-05): Verdicts of H12.1-H12.3 (Part A of the plan) in `runs/_tables/m5/verdicts.md` (and `.csv`) with the tables `h12_1` (degradations) and `h12_2` (micro comparison) and a permutation p-value in `instability_correlation`.
- D109 (2026-10-05): H1.3 on the collision-free prefix of the platoon: the growth error of an OpenACC profile compares the followers 1..min(P, c - 1) (vehicle 0 is the leader; the OpenACC platoon has P followers, 4 on Vicolungo and 9 on ZalaZone; c = the lowest follower whose gap reached 0 during the run, 51 without collision, one past the last vehicle) at which the empirical curve has a value, when there are at least 3: RMSE between the model and the empirical curve over them divided by the largest empirical value among them; fewer than 3: undefined.
- D110 (2026-10-05): Low-frequency arm of E2, penalty `kind: combined` for GRU, LSTM and PERL: the rollout gain penalty of E2 with its existence term (five frequencies 0.05-0.8 rad/s, 16 speeds, on every 8th step times 8, weight `train.penalty.weight` = 0.1) plus `train.penalty.jacobian_weight` times the Jacobian part on every training step: the terms of the Jacobian penalty of the specification (`relu(0.5 - M) + relu(-f_s) + relu(f_dv) + relu(f_v + f_dv)`, mean over the equilibria anchored to the band as in E2) with the derivatives of the memoryless view (window filled with the constant state, derivatives with respect to a common shift of the whole window = sums over the window positions, the static gains), plus the needle guard `relu(|f_dv| - c) + relu(|f_v| - c)`, `c = train.penalty.guard` = 3 1/s, inside the Jacobian part (it shares its weight and needs `jacobian_weight > 0`).
- D111 (2026-10-05): Residual-amplitude sweep of the certified hybrid: E4 repeated at `r_max` = 0.1, 0.2 and 0.5 m/s^2, `e4_stable_r{r}` (`follownet_highd`, ResidualIDM, core with margin 0.2, certificate enforced: `lipschitz = 0.9 b / r_max` with the admissible budget `b` at that `r_max`, D86) and `e4_stable_ft_r{r}` (fine-tuned on `ngsim_i80` from the run of the same fold, seed and amplitude), five folds x five seeds each; control `e4_free_r0.3` (free core of E1, no certificate, `lipschitz` 1 of the model config, `r_max` 0.3) and `e4_free_r0.3_ft`.
- D111 (2026-10-05): Corridor laws of the residual-amplitude sweep: `residual_idm_certified_r0.1`, `residual_idm_certified_r0.2`, `residual_idm_certified_r0.5` (members `runs/e4_stable_ft_r{r}/ngsim_i80/residual_idm/driver_fold{0..4}_seed0`) and `residual_idm_free_r0.3` (`runs/e4_free_r0.3_ft/...`) in `configs/corridor/laws.yaml`, kind `models` on the CPU as `residual_idm_certified`.
- D112 (2026-10-05): Second corridor, NGSIM US-101 (`configs/corridor/us101.yaml`; the code of I-80 generalised in `cf_stability/corridor/scenario.py`, `loop.py`, `macro.py`).
- D113 (2026-10-05): Variants of a scenario, `<site>_p<period>_<variant>`: `scripts/build_corridor.py variant=<name>` with overrides of `corridor.<site>.<key>` on the command line (`+` for a key the site config lacks) builds the periods of the sites those overrides change; `scenario.json` records `variant` and `overrides` (keys relative to the site); the name of the variant is part of its hashed config, the base scenarios keep their hashes.
- D114 (2026-10-05): Law `idm_heterogeneous_all`: one parameter set per vehicle drawn from all 7 651 per-event estimates of `ngsim_i80` (`v0` and `b` fixed, D48), the 4 811 with a parameter on a bound included, against the 2 840 interior estimates of `idm_heterogeneous`; otherwise the same law (drawing per vehicle, speed support of the `idm_global` members, CPU).

### M8 (strengthening arms)

- D115 (2026-10-07): Low-frequency expansion check (item 1 of `docs/m8_contract.md`): table and figure `lowfreq_expansion` (`cf_stability/eval/figures_supplement.py`, `python scripts/make_figures_supplement.py`).
- D116 (2026-10-07): Certificate tightness (item 2): table and figure `certificate_tightness`.
- D117 (2026-10-07): RACER-type control of E2 (item 4 of `docs/m8_contract.md`): penalty `kind: monotone` = mean over the anchored equilibria of `relu(-f_s) + relu(f_dv) + relu(f_v)`, the rational constraints of RACER (Li, Halatsis and Stern, arXiv 2312.07003) in the convention `dv = v - v_lead` (the acceleration rises with the spacing and falls with the speed difference and with the own speed), with the derivatives of the memoryless view, the sampling of the Jacobian penalty (16 speeds per epoch in 5-30 m/s, the band of the data, equilibria anchored to it, D79) and the existence term of every penalty (D74, D80; `existence_weight: 0` switches it off); no string term and no margin.
- D118 (2026-10-07): Per-event IDM cores with a stability margin and their certificate (item 5, training side).
- D118 (2026-10-07): Heterogeneous certified hybrid in the corridor: law `residual_idm_certified_het`, kind `residual_heterogeneous` (`cf_stability/corridor/laws.py`, `configs/corridor/laws.yaml`).
- D119 (2026-10-07): Band sensitivity of the audit (item 6): the stored audits hold the equilibrium spacing per speed (`s`, `status`, `n_crossings`, the band edges), but they cannot be reclassified for another band, because the anchored search of D79 (first upward crossing inside the band, else the first one of [1, 200] m) makes the equilibrium itself depend on the band: a crossing between the 1 % and the 5 % quantile becomes the equilibrium of the wider band (PERL fold 0 seed 4, 5 m/s: 18.4 -> 4.4 m), a second crossing inside the wider band replaces the first one of [1, 200] m (GRU fold 3 seed 0, 16 m/s: outside at 7.4 m -> inside at 81.2 m), and an equilibrium outside the narrower band falls back to the first crossing of [1, 200] m, whose flag the audit never computed.
- D120 (2026-10-07): Temporal hold-out on I-80 (item 7, training side): filter key `follower_prefix` of the views of D87 (the view code moved from `scripts/train.py` to `cf_stability/data/views.py` unchanged; the rule of the D87 views keeps its form, the new key enters it only when set); data view `configs/data/ngsim_i80_p0.yaml` (events and splits of `ngsim_i80`, `follower_prefix: ngsim_i80/i80/p0_`: 2 635 of 7 651 events; fold 0 keeps 1 532 / 573 / 530 training / validation / test events, filtered in all three parts).
- D120 (2026-10-07): Temporal hold-out in the corridor: laws `idm_global_p0` = kind `idm` with `calibration: data/calibration/ngsim_i80_p0/idm_global.json` (the file of `calibrate_idm.py dataset=ngsim_i80_p0 per_event=false`; one member with the `params` of the file; a `{fold}` pattern would give one per fold; a missing or ambiguous file leaves the law out with a message), support of the speeds of `idm_global`, instability by the exact gain of its parameter set; `residual_idm_certified_p0` (`runs/m8_temporal_stable_ft/ngsim_i80_p0/residual_idm/driver_fold{k}_seed0`, CPU), `mlp_p0` (CPU) and `gru_p0` (CUDA, as `gru`) from `runs/m8_temporal_ft/ngsim_i80_p0/<model>/driver_fold{k}_seed0`.
- D121 (2026-10-07): Acceleration asymmetry and oscillation spectrum (`cf_stability/corridor/asymmetry.py`, `scripts/corridor_asymmetry.py`, settings `asymmetry` of `configs/corridor_metrics.yaml`): `asymmetry.json` next to every `macro.json` (the 1 125 runs, the 75 of the sensitivity variants included, and the 11 ground truths; 20 s with 4 processes).
- D122 (2026-10-07): Pooled correlation and power (tables `correlation_pooled` and `power` of `runs/_tables/m8`, written by `scripts/corridor_metrics.py tables=true`).
- D123 (2026-10-07): Supplementary figures (items 3 and 8).
- D124 (2026-10-07): Release files.

## 10. How to reproduce

The commands of every milestone in order, from the section "Commands run" of its report; then this report.

### M1

```powershell
py -3.11 -m venv .venv
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pip install torch==2.11.0 --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cu128
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pip install numpy pandas scipy pyarrow hydra-core matplotlib pytest osqp requests

.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\download_data.py follownet openacc
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\download_data.py ngsim --locations i-80
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\download_data.py ngsim --locations us-101
# Waymo pairs: PowerShell Invoke-WebRequest, sha256 checked against the Mendeley API (decision D24)

.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\extract_events.py data=synthetic
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\extract_events.py data=openacc
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\extract_events.py data=follownet_highd
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\extract_events.py data=follownet_ngsim_i80
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\extract_events.py data=follownet_waymo
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\extract_events.py data=waymo
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\describe_events.py openacc follownet_highd follownet_ngsim_i80 follownet_waymo waymo

# for each <name> of the five event sets
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\calibrate_idm.py dataset=<name>
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\persistence_baseline.py dataset=<name>

.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest          # 136 passed
```

### M2

```powershell
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest                       # 231 passed (169 s on CPU)

# reference runs: every model, FollowNet HighD, driver split, fold 0, seed 0
powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File scripts\run_m2.ps1
#   = for each <model>:
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\train.py data=follownet_highd model=<model> fold=0 seed=0 experiment=m2

# weight of the one-step acceleration term
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\train.py data=follownet_highd model=residual_idm experiment=m2_acc0 train.acc_weight=0.0
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\train.py data=follownet_highd model=residual_idm experiment=m2_acc01 train.acc_weight=0.1
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\train.py data=follownet_highd model=mlp experiment=m2_acc0 train.acc_weight=0.0
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\train.py data=follownet_highd model=gru experiment=m2_acc0 train.acc_weight=0.0
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\train.py data=follownet_highd model=residual_idm experiment=m2_epoch0
```

### M3

```bash
./.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m pytest                       # 306 tests: 300 passed, 6 skipped (146 s)

# trainings with a penalty (lambda = 1), reference fold and seed
python scripts/train.py data=follownet_highd fold=0 seed=0 model=mlp          train.penalty.kind=jacobian    train.penalty.weight=1.0 experiment=m3_jacobian
python scripts/train.py data=follownet_highd fold=0 seed=0 model=residual_idm train.penalty.kind=jacobian    train.penalty.weight=1.0 experiment=m3_jacobian
python scripts/train.py data=follownet_highd fold=0 seed=0 model=mlp          train.penalty.kind=linear_gain train.penalty.weight=1.0 experiment=m3_linear_gain
python scripts/train.py data=follownet_highd fold=0 seed=0 model=gru          train.penalty.kind=linear_gain train.penalty.weight=1.0 experiment=m3_linear_gain
python scripts/train.py data=follownet_highd fold=0 seed=0 model=lstm         train.penalty.kind=linear_gain train.penalty.weight=1.0 experiment=m3_linear_gain

# audit of the penalised models and of the reference models of M2 (stability.json next to every model.pt)
python scripts/audit_stability.py experiment=m3_jacobian    data=follownet_highd force=true
python scripts/audit_stability.py experiment=m3_linear_gain data=follownet_highd force=true
python scripts/audit_stability.py experiment=m2_acc0        data=follownet_highd force=true
python scripts/audit_stability.py experiment=m2             data=follownet_highd force=true
```

### M4

```bash
./.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m pytest                     # 412 tests: 412 passed, 24 skipped (GPU variants)

# previews on the reference fold (fold 0, seed 0), before the experiments
python scripts/run_experiment.py configs/queue/preview.yaml            # anchored penalties, 5 runs
python scripts/run_experiment.py configs/queue/e4_pilot.yaml           # residual amplitude of the certified hybrid, 4 runs
python scripts/run_experiment.py configs/queue/preview_gain.yaml       # rollout penalty, 3 runs
python scripts/run_experiment.py configs/queue/preview_existence.yaml  # stand-in for the existence term, 2 runs

# experiments (restartable queues: complete steps are skipped, a second call retries failed jobs)
python scripts/run_experiment.py configs/queue/e1.yaml                 # 175 jobs
python scripts/run_experiment.py configs/queue/e2_sweep.yaml           # 120 jobs
python scripts/make_tables.py tables=[e2_sweep]                        # chosen weights (D85) -> e2_chosen.yaml, e5_penalised.yaml
python scripts/run_experiment.py configs/queue/e2_chosen.yaml          # 150 jobs
python scripts/run_experiment.py configs/queue/e2_existence.yaml       # 15 jobs
python scripts/run_experiment.py configs/queue/e4.yaml                 # 100 jobs
python scripts/run_experiment.py configs/queue/ngsim_idm.yaml          # 5 jobs
python scripts/run_experiment.py configs/queue/e5.yaml                 # 40 jobs
python scripts/run_experiment.py configs/queue/e5_penalised.yaml       # 30 jobs
python scripts/make_tables.py                                          # all tables and verdicts
```

### M5

```bash
./.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m pip install libsumo==1.27.1 eclipse-sumo==1.27.1   # E9, new packages only
./.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m pytest                                              # 475 tests: 475 passed, 20 skipped

python scripts/run_experiment.py configs/queue/m5_laws.yaml     # 40 jobs: laws fine-tuned on NGSIM I-80 (D97)
python scripts/build_corridor.py                                # scenarios i80_p0, i80_p1, i80_p2 and the 13 law files
python scripts/run_corridor.py scenarios=[i80_p0,i80_p1,i80_p2] laws=[...13 laws...] seeds=[0,...,9] workers=4   # 390 runs
python scripts/corridor_metrics.py                              # macro.json of every run and of the ground truth
python scripts/corridor_metrics.py compute=false tables=true    # runs/_tables/m5/
```

## 11. Supplement (M8)

Tables of `runs/_tables/m8/` (CSV and LaTeX in `tables/`) and the figures of `supplement/figures/` with their captions (docs/m8_contract.md). The loophole table of the theory notes is a document (`docs/theory_notes.md`), not a generated table.

#### Low-frequency expansion of the gain (D115)

- Low-frequency expansion of the gain (D115): fold-0 runs (seed 0, follownet_highd) of E1 and of the chosen E2 weight (D85, chosen_weights.json; idm: E1 only). Per grid speed with an equilibrium (status ok, multiple or outside: inside or outside the band) the numerical gain of the audit (stability.json, audit.equilibria[].gain) at the 3 lowest audit frequencies (0.02, 0.02423, 0.02936 rad/s) against the expansion |G| = sqrt(max(0, 1 - ω² M / f_s²)), M = f_v² + 2 f_v f_dv - 2 f_s, from the stored partial derivatives f_s, f_dv, f_v (the memoryless view: derivatives summed over the window, for GRU, LSTM and PERL; D66).

#### Relative width of the spacing band and per-driver dispersion (D123)

- Relative width of the spacing band and per-driver dispersion (D123). Spacing band of the data (D78, D123 part a): per grid speed the 5 %, 50 % and 95 % quantiles of the spacing of the near-steady samples of all events of the set (|dv| < 0.5 m/s, |a| < 0.3 m/s², speed within 0.5 m/s of the grid speed; cf_stability.train.tensors.spacing_band, dv = v - v_lead); a speed with fewer than 200 such samples has no band. relative width = (s95 - s5) / s50.

#### E2 control: monotonicity terms only (D117)

- Penalty kind monotone (relu(-f_s) + relu(f_dv) + relu(f_v) at the anchored equilibria of E2, no string term) next to E1 and the chosen E2 weight, fold 0, seed 0: spacing RMSE of the test part (m, unit driver) and its change against E1 paired over the drivers, band shares of the audit, unstable among equilibria, largest measured gain.

Sentences for the paper:

- On I-80, the seed-to-seed standard deviation of the macro error within a scenario is 0.0144 for idm_global and 0.0461 for residual_idm_certified (of the paired difference: 0.0480); detecting a 10 % difference (0.0154) between two laws at alpha 0.05 with power 0.8, paired by seed, needs 79 seeds in one scenario or 27 seeds per scenario over the 3 scenarios of the corridor; the 10 seeds per scenario of the design detect 16.6 % with power 0.8 (power 0.39 for 10 %).
- On I-80, the seed-to-seed standard deviation of the throughput (veh/h) within a scenario is 11.5 for idm_global and 14.0 for residual_idm_certified (of the paired difference: 17.1); detecting a 10 % difference (728.5) between two laws at alpha 0.05 with power 0.8, paired by seed, needs 2 seeds in one scenario or 2 seeds per scenario over the 3 scenarios of the corridor; the 10 seeds per scenario of the design detect 0.1 % with power 0.8 (power 1.00 for 10 %).
- On I-80, the seed-to-seed standard deviation of the travel time mean (s) within a scenario is 0.36 for idm_global and 0.30 for residual_idm_certified (of the paired difference: 0.43); detecting a 10 % difference (6.84) between two laws at alpha 0.05 with power 0.8, paired by seed, needs 3 seeds in one scenario or 2 seeds per scenario over the 3 scenarios of the corridor; the 10 seeds per scenario of the design detect 0.3 % with power 0.8 (power 1.00 for 10 %).
- On US-101, the seed-to-seed standard deviation of the macro error within a scenario is 0.0639 for idm_global and 0.0155 for residual_idm_certified (of the paired difference: 0.0647); detecting a 10 % difference (0.0123) between two laws at alpha 0.05 with power 0.8, paired by seed, needs 219 seeds in one scenario or 73 seeds per scenario over the 3 scenarios of the corridor; the 10 seeds per scenario of the design detect 27.9 % with power 0.8 (power 0.17 for 10 %).
- On US-101, the seed-to-seed standard deviation of the throughput (veh/h) within a scenario is 24.2 for idm_global and 8.0 for residual_idm_certified (of the paired difference: 21.1); detecting a 10 % difference (762.9) between two laws at alpha 0.05 with power 0.8, paired by seed, needs 2 seeds in one scenario or 2 seeds per scenario over the 3 scenarios of the corridor; the 10 seeds per scenario of the design detect 0.1 % with power 0.8 (power 1.00 for 10 %).
- On US-101, the seed-to-seed standard deviation of the travel time mean (s) within a scenario is 0.31 for idm_global and 0.09 for residual_idm_certified (of the paired difference: 0.32); detecting a 10 % difference (6.08) between two laws at alpha 0.05 with power 0.8, paired by seed, needs 3 seeds in one scenario or 3 seeds per scenario over the 3 scenarios of the corridor; the 10 seeds per scenario of the design detect 0.3 % with power 0.8 (power 1.00 for 10 %).

#### H12.3 with clustered inference: laws and architecture families as units (review of M8)

- H12.3 with clustered inference: laws and architecture families as units (review of M8). Clustered inference of H12.3 (review of M8): the rows of correlation_pooled, one per law and corridor (the share of unstable equilibria among the equilibria of the law's members against its mean macro error over its runs; correlation_pooled_laws), are not independent: a law has the same instability on both corridors, and the variants of one architecture (penalised, certified, residual amplitudes) are related. Spearman: as correlation_pooled (ranks within every corridor scaled to (0, 1), Pearson of the pooled ranks; per corridor: the Spearman correlation over its laws).

#### Exact a priori certificate and existence of the equilibria (M9)

- Exact a priori certificate and existence of the equilibria (M9). Runs: every run directory runs/<experiment>/<data>/<model>/<run> whose certificate.json has an a_priori entry (ResidualIDM; 334 runs of 18 experiments, 8684 (run, grid speed) cells; 25 files without it left out: 25 x no a_priori entry (model mlp)). data: the event set of the runs; r_max (m/s²): the amplitude of the residual; runs: runs of the experiment; grid speeds: speeds of per_speed per run (5, 6, ..., 30 m/s). Inputs: the stored IDM core (core.params, δ = 4 as in ResidualIDM), residual.r_max, residual.bounds (B_s, B_dv, B_v) and per_speed; no model is loaded, nothing is retrained (CPU, float64). design: core with margin 0.2 = calibrated with stability_margin 0.2, free core = without it; certified budget = the budget of D86 enforced in training (lipschitz = 0.9 b / r_max with the admissible budget b of the core), no certificate = lipschitz 1 of the model config; fine-tuned runs keep the core and budget of their source run.

#### Full-history linear analysis of the recurrent laws (M9)

- Full-history linear analysis of the recurrent laws (M9). Runs: GRU, LSTM and PERL; arms E1: gru e1, lstm e1, perl e1; E2: gru e2_gain_w0.1, lstm e2_gain_w0.1, perl e2_gain_w0.1; E2 combined: gru e2_combined_j0.1, lstm e2_combined_j0.1, perl e2_combined_j1; E2 long window: gru e2_gain_long_w0.1, lstm e2_gain_long_w0.1; E5 ACC: gru e5, lstm e5 on openacc_acc; E5 ACC penalised: gru e5_gain_w0.1, lstm e5_gain_w0.1 on openacc_acc; E5 human: gru e5, lstm e5 on openacc_human; E5 human penalised: gru e5_gain_w0.1, lstm e5_gain_w0.1 on openacc_human; on follownet_highd where no view is named (E1 and E2 five folds x five seeds, the combined arm and E5 five folds x seed 0, the long window fold 0, seed 0). E2 long window: the gain penalty of E2 (weight 0.1) measured over the last 252 s of a 380-s rollout, the pilot of the revision; E5: the control on the OpenACC views (D87) without penalty and with the chosen gain weight 0.1. Speeds: every grid speed with an equilibrium in the stored audit (status ok, multiple or outside: inside or outside the band), at the stored equilibrium; part all = every such speed, support = those in the speed range of the training data.

#### Threshold sensitivity of the audit's numerical rule (M9)

- Threshold sensitivity of the audit's numerical rule (M9). Runs: every E1 run on follownet_highd (e1: the learned models five folds x five seeds, the baselines five folds x seed 0); the stored audits (stability.json, threshold 1.02), no new rollouts. Per run and threshold the numerical flag of every equilibrium is recomputed from the stored largest gain over the 25 audit frequencies and the summary by the audit's own summarise_audit.

![lowfreq_expansion](supplement/figures/lowfreq_expansion.png)

Figure `lowfreq_expansion`: Low-frequency expansion of the gain (D115). Fold-0 runs of E1 (no penalty) and of the chosen E2 weight (D85; idm: E1 only) on follownet_highd, seed 0: at every grid speed with an equilibrium, the numerical gain |G| of the audit (stability.json) at the lowest audit frequency 0.02 rad/s (y) against the expansion |G| = sqrt(max(0, 1 - ω² M / f_s²)), M = f_v² + 2 f_v f_dv - 2 f_s, from the stored partial derivatives (memoryless view for GRU, LSTM and PERL) (x); open markers: equilibria that are not locally stable in the memoryless view (no stationary response). Solid line: numerical = expansion; dotted: |G| = 1 (points in the upper left and lower right quadrants have a different sign of |G| - 1). Speeds whose rollout at that frequency was clipped, stopped or collided are left out (16 of 324). Numbers at the three lowest frequencies: table lowfreq_expansion. ([PDF](supplement/figures/lowfreq_expansion.pdf)).

![certificate_tightness](supplement/figures/certificate_tightness.png)

Figure `certificate_tightness`: Tightness of the certificate of E4 (D116): per grid speed of every run of the certified hybrid (ResidualIDM with the margin core and the certified budget; e4_stable and e4_stable_r{0.1, 0.2, 0.5} on HighD before fine-tuning, e4_stable_ft and e4_stable_ft_r{0.1, 0.2, 0.5} on NGSIM I-80 after; 5 folds x 5 seeds each) the audited margin M of the hybrid (analytic criterion of the audit; y) against the certified lower bound of certificate.json (x). Columns: residual amplitude r_max; top row: the a priori bound (every speed with an equilibrium), bottom row: the bound at the anchored equilibria. Line: margin = bound; a point below it would violate the certificate (0 of 10068 points). Table certificate_tightness: slack per r_max, stage and bound. ([PDF](supplement/figures/certificate_tightness.pdf)).

![band_width](supplement/figures/band_width.png)

Figure `band_width`: The price of a common equilibrium (D123 part a): per grid speed, the relative width (s95 - s5) / s50 of the spacing band of the data (D78: quantiles of the near-steady samples, |dv| < 0.5 m/s, |a| < 0.3 m/s², within 0.5 m/s of the speed; all events of the set, speeds with at least 200 samples) and the standard deviation over the drivers with at least 20 near-steady samples at that speed of their median near-steady spacing, divided by s50 (open squares: fewer than 10 drivers). Left: follownet_highd, right: ngsim_i80. follownet_highd has no driver ids: every 15-s event is its own driver (D19). Numbers: table band_width. ([PDF](supplement/figures/band_width.pdf)).

![full_history_gain](supplement/figures/full_history_gain.png)

Figure `full_history_gain`: Full-history linearisation of the recurrent laws against the audit (M9). Every grid speed with an equilibrium of every E1 run (no penalty) and E2 run (gain penalty, chosen weight 0.1) of GRU, LSTM and PERL on follownet_highd (five folds x five seeds): the gain of the follower speed at 0.02 rad/s, the lowest audit frequency, from the numerical rollout of the audit (y) against the gain |G(e^(iω dt))| of the linearised two-vehicle loop with the whole 30-state history (x; the history Jacobian of the network, semi-implicit Euler at 0.1 s, transfer_windowed). Filled markers: all 31 closed-loop poles inside the unit circle; open markers: a pole on or outside it (the loop is not locally stable, the rollout grows instead of settling and the two gains need not agree; 900 of 3717 points). Axes per panel over the range of its windowed gains; 252 numerical gains above the upper limit and 54 below the lower one are drawn on the edge as triangles. Solid line: numerical = windowed; dotted: |G| = 1. Where all poles are inside and the rollout is not clipped, stopped or collided, |numerical - windowed| has median / 90 % quantile (E1) GRU 8e-05 / 9e-04, LSTM 1e-04 / 2e-03, PERL 4e-05 / 1e-03. 21 of these 2815 points (E1 and E2) differ by more than 0.05; their rollouts are not sinusoidal (fit residual of the audit 0.07 or more, against a median of 1e-03 elsewhere): a finite-amplitude response, the gap moving by |1 - G| A / ω with A / ω = 10 m at A = 0.2 m/s and 0.02 rad/s. Share of the speeds with all poles inside, E1 / E2 (mean over the runs): GRU 0.96 / 0.74, LSTM 0.90 / 0.98, PERL 0.86 / 0.12. The combined arm (D110) and the intervals: table full_history (runs/_tables/m9). ([PDF](supplement/figures/full_history_gain.pdf)).

Figure `stability_map_gru`: Equilibria of the GRU of fold 0 (follownet_highd, seed 0) on the speed grid: E1, no penalty (e1) and E2, gain penalty, weight 0.1 (e2_gain_w0.1), one panel each. Grey strip: spacing band of the training data of the fold (5-95 % quantiles of the near-steady samples, D78; linear between the grid speeds), dashed: its median (metrics.json); points: the equilibrium of every grid speed (searched inside the band where the speed has one, D79), coloured by the status of the audit with the numerical rule (gain above 1.02): stable or unstable inside the band, outside the band (the first equilibrium of 1-200 m), none (no equilibrium: triangle at the top of the panel); open markers: speeds without band; shaded: speeds outside the 1-99 % speed range of the training data. Log spacing axis. Speeds by status over both panels: none 4, unstable 33, outside 15. ([PDF](supplement/figures/stability_map_gru.pdf)).
